Rather than a productivity bust or existential risk, we identify a distribution-and-contract mismatch: AI-generated abundance coexists with demand deficiency because economic institutions are anchored to human cognitive scarcity. Three mechanisms formalize this channel. First, a displacement spiral with competing reinstatement effects: each firm's rational decision to substitute AI for labor reduces aggregate labor income, which reduces aggregate demand, accelerating further AI adoption. We derive conditions on the AI capability growth rate, diffusion speed, and reinstatement rate under which the net feedback is self-limiting versus explosive. Second, Ghost GDP: when AI-generated output substitutes for labor-generated output, monetary velocity declines monotonically in the labor share absent compensating transfers, creating a wedge between measured output and consumption-relevant income. Third, intermediation collapse: AI agents that reduce information frictions compress intermediary margins toward pure logistics costs, triggering repricing across SaaS, payments, consulting, insurance, and financial advisory.
  Because top-quintile earners drive 47--65\% of U.S.\ consumption and face the highest AI exposure, the transmission into private credit (\$2.5 trillion globally) and mortgage markets (\$13 trillion) is disproportionate. We derive eleven testable predictions with explicit falsification conditions. Calibrated simulations disciplined by FRED time series and BLS occupation-level data quantify conditions under which stable adjustment transitions to explosive crisis.

</details>


### [190] [Cognitively Layered Data Synthesis for Domain Adaptation of LLMs to Space Situational Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09231)
*Ding Linghu,Cheng Wang,Da Fan,Wei Shi,Kaifeng Yin,Xiaoliang Xue,Fan Yang,Haiyi Ren,Cong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BD-FDG框架，通过结构化知识组织、认知分层问题建模和自动化质量控制构建高质量SFT数据集，在空间态势感知领域验证了认知分层驱动的SFT数据构建有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用任务上表现出色，但迁移到复杂工程领域（如空间态势感知）仍面临挑战，主要瓶颈在于高质量监督微调数据集的构建。现有方法存在知识覆盖不全、认知深度不足、质量可控性差等问题。

Method: 提出BD-FDG框架，包含三个机制：1) 结构化知识组织，使用知识树确保结构化语料覆盖；2) 认知分层问题建模，设计涵盖9个类别和6个认知层次（从记忆到创造）的问题生成方案；3) 自动化质量控制，应用多维评分管道确保领域严谨性和一致性。

Result: 使用BD-FDG构建了约23万样本的SSA-SFT数据集，微调Qwen3-8B得到SSA-LLM-8B。在领域测试集上，相对BLEU-1提升144%（无思考）和176%（有思考）；竞技场比较中胜率达82.21%；同时基本保持了通用基准性能（MMLU-Pro、MATH-500）。

Conclusion: 认知分层驱动的SFT数据构建是复杂工程领域LLM适应的有效范式，BD-FDG为领域特定LLM适应提供了可转移的框架，验证了结构化知识组织、认知分层和自动化质量控制在提升领域性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate exceptional performance on general-purpose tasks. however, transferring them to complex engineering domains such as space situational awareness (SSA) remains challenging owing to insufficient structural alignment with mission chains, the absence of higher-order cognitive supervision, and poor correspondence between data quality criteria and engineering specifications. The core bottleneck is the construction of high-quality supervised fine-tuning (SFT) datasets. To this end, we propose BD-FDG (Bloom's Taxonomy-based Domain-specific Fine-tuning Data Generation), a framework that addresses incomplete knowledge coverage, shallow cognitive depth, and limited quality controllability through three mechanisms: structured knowledge organization, cognitively layered question modeling, and automated quality control. The framework uses a knowledge tree to ensure structured corpus coverage, designs a question generation scheme spanning nine categories and six cognitive levels from Remember to Create to produce samples with a continuous difficulty gradient, and applies a multidimensional scoring pipeline to enforce domain rigor and consistency. Using BD-FDG, we construct SSA-SFT, a domain dataset of approximately 230K samples, and fine-tune Qwen3-8B to obtain SSA-LLM-8B. Experiments show that SSA-LLM-8B achieves relative BLEU-1 improvements of 144\% (no-think) and 176\% (think) on the domain test set and a win rate of 82.21\% over the baseline in arena comparisons, while largely preserving general benchmark performance (MMLU-Pro, MATH-500). These results validate SFT data construction driven by cognitive layering as an effective paradigm for complex engineering domains and provide a transferable framework for domain-specific LLM adaptation.

</details>


### [191] [Social-R1: Towards Human-like Social Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09249)
*Jincenzi Wu,Yuxuan Lei,Jianxun Lian,Yitian Huang,Lexin Zhou,Haotian Li,Xing Xie,Helen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出ToMBench-Hard对抗性基准和Social-R1强化学习框架，通过挑战性训练案例和轨迹级对齐，使4B参数模型在社交推理任务上超越更大模型并实现鲁棒泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在社交智能方面仍面临挑战，当前模型依赖表面模式而非真正的社交推理。为培养类人社交智能，需要能够抵抗捷径解决方案的挑战性训练案例。

Method: 1) 引入ToMBench-Hard对抗性基准，提供社交推理的困难训练示例；2) 提出Social-R1强化学习框架，通过多维奖励将模型推理与人类认知对齐，监督整个推理过程，强制执行结构对齐、逻辑完整性和信息密度。

Result: 4B参数模型超越了更大的模型，在八个多样化基准上实现了鲁棒泛化，证明了挑战性训练案例与轨迹级对齐的有效性。

Conclusion: 挑战性训练案例与轨迹级对齐为高效可靠的社交智能提供了一条可行路径，使较小模型能够超越更大模型并实现鲁棒泛化。

Abstract: While large language models demonstrate remarkable capabilities across numerous domains, social intelligence - the capacity to perceive social cues, infer mental states, and generate appropriate responses - remains a critical challenge, particularly for enabling effective human-AI collaboration and developing AI that truly serves human needs. Current models often rely on superficial patterns rather than genuine social reasoning. We argue that cultivating human-like social intelligence requires training with challenging cases that resist shortcut solutions. To this end, we introduce ToMBench-Hard, an adversarial benchmark designed to provide hard training examples for social reasoning. Building on this, we propose Social-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that aligns model reasoning with human cognition through multi-dimensional rewards. Unlike outcome-based RL, Social-R1 supervises the entire reasoning process, enforcing structural alignment, logical integrity, and information density. Results show that our approach enables a 4B parameter model to surpass much larger counterparts and generalize robustly across eight diverse benchmarks. These findings demonstrate that challenging training cases with trajectory-level alignment offer a path toward efficient and reliable social intelligence.

</details>


### [192] [Logos: An evolvable reasoning engine for rational molecular design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09268)
*Haibin Wen,Zhe Zhao,Fanfu Wang,Tianyi Xu,Hao Zhang,Chao Yang,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Logos：一种紧凑的分子推理模型，通过多步逻辑推理与严格化学一致性相结合，实现可靠且可解释的分子设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习模型在分子性质预测和候选生成方面存在局限性：要么具有物理保真度但缺乏透明推理，要么具有灵活推理但缺乏化学有效性保证。这种不平衡限制了AI系统在实际科学设计工作流程中的可靠性。

Method: 采用分阶段训练策略：1) 首先让模型接触将分子描述与结构决策联系起来的显式推理示例；2) 逐步将这些推理模式与分子表示对齐；3) 在最终训练阶段，将化学规则和不变性直接纳入优化目标，引导模型产生化学有效输出。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Logos在结构准确性和化学有效性方面均表现出色，匹配或超越了参数规模大得多的通用语言模型。在涉及多个潜在冲突约束的分子优化任务中表现出稳定行为。通过显式展示中间推理步骤，支持人类对设计逻辑的检查和评估。

Conclusion: 联合优化推理结构和物理一致性为分子科学提供了可靠且可解释的AI系统的实用途径，支持人工智能更紧密地融入科学发现过程。

Abstract: The discovery and design of functional molecules remain central challenges across chemistry,biology, and materials science. While recent advances in machine learning have accelerated molecular property prediction and candidate generation, existing models tend to excel either in physical fidelity without transparent reasoning, or in flexible reasoning without guarantees of chemical validity. This imbalance limits the reliability of artificial intelligence systems in real scientific design workflows.Here we present Logos, a compact molecular reasoning model that integrates multi-step logical reasoning with strict chemical consistency. Logos is trained using a staged strategy that first exposes the model to explicit reasoning examples linking molecular descriptions to structural decisions, and then progressively aligns these reasoning patterns with molecular representations. In a final training phase, chemical rules and invariants are incorporated directly into the optimization objective, guiding the model toward chemically valid outputs. Across multiple benchmark datasets, Logos achieves strong performance in both structural accuracy and chemical validity, matching or surpassing substantially larger general-purpose language models while operating with a fraction of their parameters. Beyond benchmark evaluation, the model exhibits stable behaviour in molecular optimization tasks involving multiple, potentially conflicting constraints. By explicitly exposing intermediate reasoning steps, Logos enables human inspection and assessment of the design logic underlying each generated structure. These results indicate that jointly optimizing for reasoning structure and physical consistency offers a practical pathway toward reliable and interpretable AI systems for molecular science, supporting closer integration of artificial intelligence into scientific discovery processes.

</details>


### [193] [Rescaling Confidence: What Scale Design Reveals About LLM Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09309)
*Yuyang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM口头置信度（0-100）存在严重离散化问题，78%以上响应集中在三个整数上。通过系统操纵置信度尺度的三个维度（粒度、边界位置、范围规律性），发现0-20尺度比标准0-100格式能提高元认知效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM口头置信度（报告数值确定性分数）被广泛用于黑盒设置中的不确定性估计，但置信度尺度本身（通常是0-100）很少被检验。研究者发现这一设计选择并非中性，需要系统研究尺度设计如何影响口头不确定性的质量。

Method: 在六个LLM和三个数据集上分析口头置信度的分布模式。系统操纵置信度尺度的三个维度：粒度（精细程度）、边界位置（尺度端点）和范围规律性（是否均匀分布）。使用元d'（meta-d'）评估元认知敏感性，比较不同尺度设计下的表现。

Result: 口头置信度存在严重离散化，超过78%的响应集中在三个整数上。0-20尺度在元认知效率上持续优于标准0-100格式。边界压缩会降低性能，整数偏好即使在非均匀范围下仍然存在。置信度尺度设计直接影响口头不确定性的质量。

Conclusion: 置信度尺度设计是影响LLM口头不确定性质量的关键因素，应被视为LLM评估中的首要实验变量。标准0-100尺度并非最优，更精细的尺度设计（如0-20）能提高元认知效率。

Abstract: Verbalized confidence, in which LLMs report a numerical certainty score, is widely used to estimate uncertainty in black-box settings, yet the confidence scale itself (typically 0--100) is rarely examined. We show that this design choice is not neutral. Across six LLMs and three datasets, verbalized confidence is heavily discretized, with more than 78% of responses concentrating on just three round-number values. To investigate this phenomenon, we systematically manipulate confidence scales along three dimensions: granularity, boundary placement, and range regularity, and evaluate metacognitive sensitivity using meta-d'. We find that a 0--20 scale consistently improves metacognitive efficiency over the standard 0--100 format, while boundary compression degrades performance and round-number preferences persist even under irregular ranges. These results demonstrate that confidence scale design directly affects the quality of verbalized uncertainty and should be treated as a first-class experimental variable in LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [194] [Curveball Steering: The Right Direction To Steer Isn't Always Linear](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09313)
*Shivam Raval,Hae Jin Song,Linlin Wu,Abir Harrasse,Jeff Phillips,Amirali Abdullah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文质疑线性表示假设，发现LLM激活空间存在显著几何扭曲，提出基于多项式核PCA的非线性控制方法Curveball steering，在几何扭曲强的场景中优于线性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于线性表示假设的激活控制方法在实践中表现不一致，作者质疑该假设的合理性，通过分析LLM激活空间的内在几何特性来探究非线性控制的可能性。

Method: 通过测地距离与欧氏距离之比测量几何扭曲程度，发现激活空间存在概念依赖的显著扭曲。提出Curveball steering方法，使用多项式核PCA在特征空间中进行非线性干预，更好地尊重学习到的激活几何结构。

Result: 观察到激活空间存在实质性的几何扭曲，表明全局线性几何近似不准确。Curveball steering在几何扭曲强的场景中一致优于基于线性PCA的控制方法。

Conclusion: 几何感知的非线性控制为全局线性干预提供了原则性替代方案，激活空间的非线性几何特性需要被纳入控制方法设计中。

Abstract: Activation steering is a widely used approach for controlling large language model (LLM) behavior by intervening on internal representations. Existing methods largely rely on the Linear Representation Hypothesis, assuming behavioral attributes can be manipulated using global linear directions. In practice, however, such linear interventions often behave inconsistently. We question this assumption by analyzing the intrinsic geometry of LLM activation spaces. Measuring geometric distortion via the ratio of geodesic to Euclidean distances, we observe substantial and concept-dependent distortions, indicating that activation spaces are not well-approximated by a globally linear geometry. Motivated by this, we propose "Curveball steering", a nonlinear steering method based on polynomial kernel PCA that performs interventions in a feature space, better respecting the learned activation geometry. Curveball steering consistently outperforms linear PCA-based steering, particularly in regimes exhibiting strong geometric distortion, suggesting that geometry-aware, nonlinear steering provides a principled alternative to global, linear interventions.

</details>


### [195] [Robust Regularized Policy Iteration under Transition Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09344)
*Hongqiang Lin,Zhenghui Fu,Weihao Tang,Pengfei Wang,Yiding Sun,Qixian Huang,Dongxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RRPI提出了一种鲁棒正则化策略迭代方法，通过将离线RL建模为鲁棒策略优化问题，在不确定性集合内处理转移核不确定性，使用KL正则化替代难以求解的双层目标，实现了对最坏情况动态的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在分布偏移下性能下降，学习策略可能访问值估计和学习动态不可靠的分布外状态-动作对。需要解决策略诱导的外推和转移不确定性，在统一框架中处理这些问题。

Method: 将离线RL建模为鲁棒策略优化，将转移核作为不确定性集合内的决策变量，针对最坏情况动态优化策略。提出鲁棒正则化策略迭代(RRPI)，用可处理的KL正则化替代难以求解的最大-最小双层目标，基于鲁棒正则化贝尔曼算子推导高效策略迭代过程。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，RRPI实现了强大的平均性能，在大多数环境中优于包括PMDB等基于百分位数方法的最新基线，同时在其余环境中保持竞争力。学习到的Q值在认知不确定性较高的区域下降，表明所得策略在转移不确定性下避免了不可靠的分布外动作。

Conclusion: RRPI通过鲁棒正则化策略迭代有效解决了离线RL中的分布偏移问题，提供了理论保证和实证优势，在不确定性下实现了鲁棒的行为，避免了不可靠的分布外动作。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) enables data-efficient and safe policy learning without online exploration, but its performance often degrades under distribution shift. The learned policy may visit out-of-distribution state-action pairs where value estimates and learned dynamics are unreliable. To address policy-induced extrapolation and transition uncertainty in a unified framework, we formulate offline RL as robust policy optimization, treating the transition kernel as a decision variable within an uncertainty set and optimizing the policy against the worst-case dynamics. We propose Robust Regularized Policy Iteration (RRPI), which replaces the intractable max-min bilevel objective with a tractable KL-regularized surrogate and derives an efficient policy iteration procedure based on a robust regularized Bellman operator. We provide theoretical guarantees by showing that the proposed operator is a $γ$-contraction and that iteratively updating the surrogate yields monotonic improvement of the original robust objective with convergence. Experiments on D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that RRPI achieves strong average performance, outperforming recent baselines including percentile-based methods such as PMDB on the majority of environments while remaining competitive on the rest. Moreover, RRPI exhibits robust behavior. The learned $Q$-values decrease in regions with higher epistemic uncertainty, suggesting that the resulting policy avoids unreliable out-of-distribution actions under transition uncertainty.

</details>


### [196] [AI Act Evaluation Benchmark: An Open, Transparent, and Reproducible Evaluation Dataset for NLP and RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09435)
*Athanasios Davvetas,Michael Papademas,Xenia Ziouvelou,Vangelis Karkaletsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创建资源的方法，用于评估NLP模型（特别是RAG系统）对欧盟AI法案的合规性，通过生成包含风险分类、条款检索、义务生成和问答任务的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在公共和社会领域的快速部署增加了对监管标准合规性的需求，但缺乏资源阻碍了对AI系统合规性的半自动或自动评估，导致需要易出错、资源有限的人工工作。

Method: 开发了一种开放、透明、可复现的方法，结合领域知识和大型语言模型的处理推理能力，生成包含风险级别分类、条款检索、义务生成和问答任务的EU AI Act数据集，采用机器对机器友好的格式。

Result: 创建了包含风险分类、条款检索、义务生成和问答任务的EU AI Act数据集，能够处理法规中未明确定义的风险级别边界（如有限和最小风险案例），并通过RAG系统评估显示在禁止和高风险场景下分别达到0.87和0.85的F1分数。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了如何利用语言模型进行基于文档的高相关性生成，为评估NLP模型对监管框架的合规性提供了有效资源，特别适用于RAG系统，解决了法规中模糊边界的问题。

Abstract: The rapid rollout of AI in heterogeneous public and societal sectors has subsequently escalated the need for compliance with regulatory standards and frameworks. The EU AI Act has emerged as a landmark in the regulatory landscape. The development of solutions that elicit the level of AI systems' compliance with such standards is often limited by the lack of resources, hindering the semi-automated or automated evaluation of their performance. This generates the need for manual work, which is often error-prone, resource-limited or limited to cases not clearly described by the regulation. This paper presents an open, transparent, and reproducible method of creating a resource that facilitates the evaluation of NLP models with a strong focus on RAG systems. We have developed a dataset that contain the tasks of risk-level classification, article retrieval, obligation generation, and question-answering for the EU AI Act. The dataset files are in a machine-to-machine appropriate format. To generate the files, we utilise domain knowledge as an exegetical basis, combining with the processing and reasoning power of large language models to generate scenarios along with the respective tasks. Our methodology demonstrates a way to harness language models for grounded generation with high document relevancy. Besides, we overcome limitations such as navigating the decision boundaries of risk-levels that are not explicitly defined within the EU AI Act, such as limited and minimal cases. Finally, we demonstrate our dataset's effectiveness by evaluating a RAG-based solution that reaches 0.87 and 0.85 F1-score for prohibited and high-risk scenarios.

</details>


### [197] [An Empirical Study and Theoretical Explanation on Task-Level Model-Merging Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09463)
*Yuan Cao,Dezhi Ran,Yuzhe Guo,Mengzhou Wu,Simin Chen,Linyi Li,Wei Yang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了模型合并中的"合并崩溃"现象，即某些任务组合在合并后会出现灾难性的性能下降，并发现表征不兼容性是主要因素，而非参数空间冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并能够统一从相同基础模型独立微调的LLM，实现并行开发工作的重用和集成而无需重新训练。然而在实践中观察到合并并不总是成功：某些任务专家模型的组合在合并后会遭受灾难性的性能下降。这种失败模式被称为"合并崩溃"，需要深入理解其根本原因。

Method: 通过广泛的实验和统计分析，识别并表征任务级合并崩溃现象。研究不同任务组合的合并效果，分析表征不兼容性与参数空间冲突指标之间的相关性。提供基于率失真理论的理论解释，建立维度相关的界限，确立任务可合并性的基本限制。

Result: 研究发现任务之间的表征不兼容性与合并崩溃有强相关性，而参数空间冲突指标显示最小相关性，这挑战了模型合并文献中的传统观点。某些任务组合在所有合并方法中都会一致触发巨大的性能下降。理论分析通过维度相关的界限解释了这一现象。

Conclusion: 合并崩溃的根本原因在于任务之间的表征不兼容性，而非参数空间冲突。率失真理论提供了理论解释，表明存在与维度相关的基本限制，决定了任务的可合并性，无论采用何种合并方法。这一发现对模型合并的实际应用具有重要意义。

Abstract: Model merging unifies independently fine-tuned LLMs from the same base, enabling reuse and integration of parallel development efforts without retraining. However, in practice we observe that merging does not always succeed: certain combinations of task-specialist models suffer from catastrophic performance degradation after merging. We refer to this failure mode as merging collapse. Intuitively, collapse arises when the learned representations or parameter adjustments for different tasks are fundamentally incompatible, so that merging forces destructive interference rather than synergy. In this paper, we identify and characterize the phenomenon of task-level merging collapse, where certain task combinations consistently trigger huge performance degradation across all merging methods. Through extensive experiments and statistical analysis, we demonstrate that representational incompatibility between tasks is strongly correlated with merging collapse, while parameter-space conflict metrics show minimal correlation, challenging conventional wisdom in model merging literature. We provide a theoretical explanation on this phenomenon through rate-distortion theory with a dimension-dependent bound, establishing fundamental limits on task mergeability regardless of methodology.

</details>


### [198] [Telogenesis: Goal Is All U Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09476)
*Zhuoran Deng,Yizhi Zhang,Ziyi Zhang,Wan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于认知间隙（无知、惊讶、陈旧）生成注意优先级的函数，验证了在没有外部奖励的情况下，仅凭内部认知状态就能产生自适应注意优先级，并能恢复环境潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标导向系统假设目标由外部提供，本研究探索注意优先级是否能从智能体内部认知状态内生地产生，而不依赖外部奖励信号。

Method: 提出优先级函数，基于三个认知间隙生成观察目标：无知（后验方差）、惊讶（预测误差）和陈旧（未观察变量置信度的时间衰减）。在两个系统中验证：最小注意力分配环境（2000次运行）和模块化部分可观测世界（500次运行）。进行消融实验验证各组件必要性，并让衰减率可学习。

Result: 发现度量依赖反转现象：全局预测误差下，基于覆盖的轮转策略更优；变化检测延迟下，优先级引导分配更优，优势随维度单调增长（d=-0.95，N=48，p<10^-6）。检测延迟随注意力预算呈幂律分布，优先级引导分配的指数更陡（0.55 vs 0.40）。当衰减率可学习时，系统能无监督地自发恢复环境波动结构（t=22.5，p<10^-6）。

Conclusion: 仅凭认知间隙（无需外部奖励）就足以产生自适应优先级，优于固定策略，并能恢复潜在环境结构，为内生注意机制提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned systems assume goals are provided externally. We ask whether attentional priorities can emerge endogenously from an agent's internal cognitive state. We propose a priority function that generates observation targets from three epistemic gaps: ignorance (posterior variance), surprise (prediction error), and staleness (temporal decay of confidence in unobserved variables). We validate this in two systems: a minimal attention-allocation environment (2,000 runs) and a modular, partially observable world (500 runs). Ablation shows each component is necessary. A key finding is metric-dependent reversal: under global prediction error, coverage-based rotation wins; under change detection latency, priority-guided allocation wins, with advantage growing monotonically with dimensionality (d = -0.95 at N=48, p < 10^-6). Detection latency follows a power law in attention budget, with a steeper exponent for priority-guided allocation (0.55 vs. 0.40). When the decay rate is made learnable per variable, the system spontaneously recovers environmental volatility structure without supervision (t = 22.5, p < 10^-6). We demonstrate that epistemic gaps alone, without external reward, suffice to generate adaptive priorities that outperform fixed strategies and recover latent environmental structure.

</details>


### [199] [GenePlan: Evolving Better Generalized PDDL Plans using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09481)
*Andrew Murray,Danial Dervovic,Alberto Pozanco,Michael Cashmore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GenePlan是一个利用大语言模型辅助进化算法生成PDDL描述经典规划任务的领域相关广义规划器的新框架，通过将广义规划转化为优化问题，迭代演化可解释的Python规划器以最小化不同问题实例的规划长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典规划任务中需要为每个新问题实例重新规划的低效问题，通过生成能够处理同一领域内多个问题实例的广义规划器，提高规划效率和泛化能力。

Method: 采用大语言模型辅助的进化算法框架，将广义规划转化为优化问题，迭代演化可解释的Python规划器，目标是最小化规划长度，使用PDDL描述规划任务。

Result: 在六个现有基准领域和两个新领域上，GenePlan平均SAT得分为0.91，接近最先进规划器的0.93分，显著优于其他基于LLM的基线方法（如思维链提示的平均SAT得分0.64），生成规划器解决新实例速度快（平均0.49秒/任务）且成本低（使用GPT-4o平均每领域1.82美元）。

Conclusion: GenePlan框架成功地将大语言模型与进化算法结合，能够高效生成高性能的广义规划器，在保持接近最优性能的同时显著降低了计算成本和规划时间，为自动规划领域提供了有前景的新方法。

Abstract: We present GenePlan (GENeralized Evolutionary Planner), a novel framework that leverages large language model (LLM) assisted evolutionary algorithms to generate domain-dependent generalized planners for classical planning tasks described in PDDL. By casting generalized planning as an optimization problem, GenePlan iteratively evolves interpretable Python planners that minimize plan length across diverse problem instances. In empirical evaluation across six existing benchmark domains and two new domains, GenePlan achieved an average SAT score of 0.91, closely matching the performance of the state-of-the-art planners (SAT score 0.93), and significantly outperforming other LLM-based baselines such as chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting (average SAT score 0.64). The generated planners solve new instances rapidly (average 0.49 seconds per task) and at low cost (average $1.82 per domain using GPT-4o).

</details>


### [200] [Enhancing Debunking Effectiveness through LLM-based Personality Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09533)
*Pietro Dell'Oglio,Alessandro Bondielli,Francesco Marcelloni,Lucia C. Passaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于大五人格特质的个性化假新闻辟谣信息生成方法，利用LLM将通用辟谣内容转化为针对特定人格特征的个性化版本，并通过LLM作为自动化评估器验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前假新闻辟谣信息多为通用形式，缺乏个性化针对性，导致说服效果有限。需要开发能够根据不同人格特征进行个性化调整的辟谣方法，以提高辟谣信息的说服力和有效性。

Method: 基于大五人格特质（外向性、宜人性、尽责性、神经质、开放性）构建人格特征输入提示，指导大型语言模型将通用辟谣内容转化为个性化版本。使用独立的LLM作为自动化评估器，模拟相应人格特质来评估个性化信息的说服效果，避免昂贵的人工评估。

Result: 个性化信息普遍比通用信息更具说服力。开放性特质倾向于提高说服力，而神经质特质可能降低说服力。不同LLM评估器之间的差异表明使用多个模型能提供更清晰的评估结果。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了利用LLM创建更有针对性的辟谣信息的实用途径，同时引发了关于此类技术使用方式的伦理问题。个性化辟谣信息能提高说服效果，但需要谨慎考虑其伦理影响。

Abstract: This study proposes a novel methodology for generating personalized fake news debunking messages by prompting Large Language Models (LLMs) with persona-based inputs aligned to the Big Five personality traits: Extraversion, Agreeableness, Conscientiousness, Neuroticism, and Openness. Our approach guides LLMs to transform generic debunking content into personalized versions tailored to specific personality profiles. To assess the effectiveness of these transformations, we employ a separate LLM as an automated evaluator simulating corresponding personality traits, thereby eliminating the need for costly human evaluation panels. Our results show that personalized messages are generally seen as more persuasive than generic ones. We also find that traits like Openness tend to increase persuadability, while Neuroticism can lower it. Differences between LLM evaluators suggest that using multiple models provides a clearer picture. Overall, this work demonstrates a practical way to create more targeted debunking messages exploiting LLMs, while also raising important ethical questions about how such technology might be used.

</details>


### [201] [Logics-Parsing-Omni Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09677)
*Xin An,Jingyi Cai,Xiangyang Chen,Huayao Liu,Peiting Liu,Peng Wang,Bei Yang,Xiuwen Zhu,Yongfan Chen,Baoyu Hou,Shuzhao Li,Weidong Ren,Fan Yang,Jiangtao Zhang,Xiaoxiao Xu,Lin Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Omni Parsing框架通过统一的多模态解析方法，将非结构化数据转换为可定位、可枚举、可追踪的结构化知识，实现了从感知到认知的渐进式解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态解析中任务定义碎片化和非结构化数据异质性带来的挑战，建立统一的解析框架来处理文档、图像和音视频流数据。

Method: 提出Omni Parsing框架，包含三个层次：1)整体检测实现空间-时间定位；2)细粒度识别进行符号化和属性提取；3)多级解释构建从局部语义到全局逻辑的推理链。采用证据锚定机制确保高层语义描述与低层事实对齐。

Result: 构建了标准化数据集并发布了Logics-Parsing-Omni模型，成功将复杂音视频信号转换为机器可读的结构化知识。实验表明细粒度感知与高层认知具有协同效应，有效提升了模型可靠性。同时发布了OmniParsingBench用于定量评估。

Conclusion: Omni Parsing框架通过统一的多模态解析方法，实现了从非结构化信号到结构化知识的转换，为多模态理解提供了系统化解决方案，并通过证据锚定机制确保了推理的可靠性。

Abstract: Addressing the challenges of fragmented task definitions and the heterogeneity of unstructured data in multimodal parsing, this paper proposes the Omni Parsing framework. This framework establishes a Unified Taxonomy covering documents, images, and audio-visual streams, introducing a progressive parsing paradigm that bridges perception and cognition. Specifically, the framework integrates three hierarchical levels: 1) Holistic Detection, which achieves precise spatial-temporal grounding of objects or events to establish a geometric baseline for perception; 2) Fine-grained Recognition, which performs symbolization (e.g., OCR/ASR) and attribute extraction on localized objects to complete structured entity parsing; and 3) Multi-level Interpreting, which constructs a reasoning chain from local semantics to global logic. A pivotal advantage of this framework is its evidence anchoring mechanism, which enforces a strict alignment between high-level semantic descriptions and low-level facts. This enables ``evidence-based'' logical induction, transforming unstructured signals into standardized knowledge that is locatable, enumerable, and traceable. Building on this foundation, we constructed a standardized dataset and released the Logics-Parsing-Omni model, which successfully converts complex audio-visual signals into machine-readable structured knowledge. Experiments demonstrate that fine-grained perception and high-level cognition are synergistic, effectively enhancing model reliability. Furthermore, to quantitatively evaluate these capabilities, we introduce OmniParsingBench. Code, models and the benchmark are released at https://github.com/alibaba/Logics-Parsing/tree/master/Logics-Parsing-Omni.

</details>


### [202] [OOD-MMSafe: Advancing MLLM Safety from Harmful Intent to Hidden Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09706)
*Ming Wen,Kun Yang,Jingyu Zhang,Yuxuan Liu,shiwen cui,Shouling Ji,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了面向后果驱动的安全范式，以解决多模态大语言模型在因果链中识别潜在危险的能力不足问题，并开发了CASPO框架来增强模型的安全推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型的安全对齐主要针对恶意意图或情境违规，忽视了后果驱动的安全需求，这对于自主和具身智能体的稳健部署至关重要。现有模型在识别上下文依赖的因果链中的潜在危险方面存在显著缺陷。

Method: 1. 提出OOD-MMSafe基准，包含455个精心设计的查询-图像对，用于评估模型在因果链中识别潜在危险的能力；2. 开发Consequence-Aware Safety Policy Optimization (CASPO)框架，该框架将模型的内在推理作为动态参考，用于token级别的自蒸馏奖励；3. 通过实验验证CASPO在增强后果预测能力方面的有效性。

Result: 1. 前沿模型普遍存在因果盲区，高性能闭源模型的失败率高达67.5%；2. 识别出偏好天花板现象：随着模型容量增长，静态对齐导致格式中心化失败而非安全推理改进；3. CASPO显著提升了后果预测能力，将Qwen2.5-VL-7B的风险识别失败率降至7.3%，Qwen3-VL-4B降至5.7%，同时保持整体有效性。

Conclusion: 后果驱动的安全范式对于多模态大语言模型的稳健部署至关重要。CASPO框架通过整合模型内在推理进行动态自蒸馏，有效解决了现有模型在因果链安全推理方面的瓶颈，为自主和具身智能体的安全部署提供了重要技术支撑。

Abstract: While safety alignment for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has gained significant attention, current paradigms primarily target malicious intent or situational violations. We propose shifting the safety frontier toward consequence-driven safety, a paradigm essential for the robust deployment of autonomous and embodied agents. To formalize this shift, we introduce OOD-MMSafe, a benchmark comprising 455 curated query-image pairs designed to evaluate a model's ability to identify latent hazards within context-dependent causal chains. Our analysis reveals a pervasive causal blindness among frontier models, with the highest 67.5% failure rate in high-capacity closed-source models, and identifies a preference ceiling where static alignment yields format-centric failures rather than improved safety reasoning as model capacity grows. To address these bottlenecks, we develop the Consequence-Aware Safety Policy Optimization (CASPO) framework, which integrates the model's intrinsic reasoning as a dynamic reference for token-level self-distillation rewards. Experimental results demonstrate that CASPO significantly enhances consequence projection, reducing the failure ratio of risk identification to 7.3% for Qwen2.5-VL-7B and 5.7% for Qwen3-VL-4B while maintaining overall effectiveness.

</details>


### [203] [AutoAgent: Evolving Cognition and Elastic Memory Orchestration for Adaptive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09716)
*Xiaoxing Wang,Ning Liao,Shikun Wei,Chen Tang,Feiyu Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoAgent是一个自演化的多智能体框架，通过演化认知、实时上下文决策和弹性内存编排解决传统智能体在长期经验学习和实时决策之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体框架难以平衡长期经验学习和实时上下文敏感决策，表现为静态认知、刚性工作流依赖和低效上下文使用，限制了在开放非平稳环境中的适应性。

Method: AutoAgent包含三个紧密耦合组件：1)演化认知：智能体维护工具、自身能力、同伴专长和任务知识的结构化提示级认知；2)实时上下文决策：结合实时任务上下文从统一动作空间选择行动；3)弹性内存编排器：动态组织交互历史，保留原始记录、压缩冗余轨迹、构建可重用情节抽象。

Result: 在检索增强推理、工具增强智能体基准和具身任务环境中的实验表明，AutoAgent在任务成功率、工具使用效率和协作鲁棒性方面持续优于静态和内存增强基线。

Conclusion: AutoAgent为自适应自主智能体提供了统一实用基础，能够在动态环境中从经验学习的同时做出可靠的上下文感知决策。

Abstract: Autonomous agent frameworks still struggle to reconcile long-term experiential learning with real-time, context-sensitive decision-making. In practice, this gap appears as static cognition, rigid workflow dependence, and inefficient context usage, which jointly limit adaptability in open-ended and non-stationary environments. To address these limitations, we present AutoAgent, a self-evolving multi-agent framework built on three tightly coupled components: evolving cognition, on-the-fly contextual decision-making, and elastic memory orchestration. At the core of AutoAgent, each agent maintains structured prompt-level cognition over tools, self-capabilities, peer expertise, and task knowledge. During execution, this cognition is combined with live task context to select actions from a unified space that includes tool calls, LLM-based generation, and inter-agent requests. To support efficient long-horizon reasoning, an Elastic Memory Orchestrator dynamically organizes interaction history by preserving raw records, compressing redundant trajectories, and constructing reusable episodic abstractions, thereby reducing token overhead while retaining decision-critical evidence. These components are integrated through a closed-loop cognitive evolution process that aligns intended actions with observed outcomes to continuously update cognition and expand reusable skills, without external retraining. Empirical results across retrieval-augmented reasoning, tool-augmented agent benchmarks, and embodied task environments show that AutoAgent consistently improves task success, tool-use efficiency, and collaborative robustness over static and memory-augmented baselines. Overall, AutoAgent provides a unified and practical foundation for adaptive autonomous agents that must learn from experience while making reliable context-aware decisions in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [204] [World2Mind: Cognition Toolkit for Allocentric Spatial Reasoning in Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09774)
*Shouwei Ruan,Bin Wang,Zhenyu Wu,Qihui Zhu,Yuxiang Zhang,Hang Su,Yubin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: World2Mind是一个无需训练的空间智能工具包，通过构建结构化空间认知地图增强多模态基础模型的空间推理能力，显著提升性能5%~18%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两个主要问题：1）通过3D接地数据过度拟合统计捷径；2）局限于2D视觉感知。这限制了空间推理的准确性和在未见场景中的泛化能力。受生物智能的空间认知映射机制启发，需要开发更鲁棒的空间推理方法。

Method: World2Mind利用3D重建和实例分割模型构建结构化空间认知地图，通过椭圆参数建模地标布局创建Allocentric-Spatial Tree（AST），并采用三阶段推理链：工具调用评估、模态解耦线索收集、几何-语义交织推理，以缓解3D重建的固有误差。

Result: 实验表明World2Mind将前沿模型（如GPT-5.2）的性能提升5%~18%。仅依靠AST结构化文本，纯文本基础模型就能执行复杂3D空间推理，性能接近先进多模态模型。

Conclusion: World2Mind通过构建结构化空间认知地图和引入几何-拓扑先验，有效增强了多模态基础模型的空间推理能力，实现了无需训练的鲁棒空间智能，为空间认知研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Achieving robust spatial reasoning remains a fundamental challenge for current Multimodal Foundation Models (MFMs). Existing methods either overfit statistical shortcuts via 3D grounding data or remain confined to 2D visual perception, limiting both spatial reasoning accuracy and generalization in unseen scenarios. Inspired by the spatial cognitive mapping mechanisms of biological intelligence, we propose World2Mind, a training-free spatial intelligence toolkit. At its core, World2Mind leverages 3D reconstruction and instance segmentation models to construct structured spatial cognitive maps, empowering MFMs to proactively acquire targeted spatial knowledge regarding interested landmarks and routes of interest. To provide robust geometric-topological priors, World2Mind synthesizes an Allocentric-Spatial Tree (AST) that uses elliptical parameters to model the top-down layout of landmarks accurately. To mitigate the inherent inaccuracies of 3D reconstruction, we introduce a three-stage reasoning chain comprising tool invocation assessment, modality-decoupled cue collection, and geometry-semantics interwoven reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that World2Mind boosts the performance of frontier models, such as GPT-5.2, by 5%~18%. Astonishingly, relying solely on the AST-structured text, purely text-only foundation models can perform complex 3D spatial reasoning, achieving performance approaching that of advanced multimodal models.

</details>


### [205] [Influencing LLM Multi-Agent Dialogue via Policy-Parameterized Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09890)
*Hongbo Bo,Jingyu Hu,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的多智能体系统框架，将提示视为动作，通过参数化构建轻量级策略来影响对话行为，无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体研究依赖临时性提示，缺乏原则性的策略视角。需要探索是否可以通过参数化提示作为动作来构建轻量级策略，从而影响对话行为。

Method: 将提示视为LLM执行的动作，基于智能体当前状态通过五个组件动态构建提示。在两种公共讨论场景中使用不同LLM驱动的智能体进行实验，评估参数化控制的有效性。

Result: 实验表明提示参数化能够影响对话动态，基于五个指标（响应性、反驳、证据使用、非重复性、立场转变）的评估显示参数化控制有效。

Conclusion: 策略参数化的提示提供了一种简单有效的机制来影响对话过程，有助于多智能体系统在社会模拟方向的研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a new paradigm for multi-agent systems. However, existing research on the behaviour of LLM-based multi-agents relies on ad hoc prompts and lacks a principled policy perspective. Different from reinforcement learning, we investigate whether prompt-as-action can be parameterized so as to construct a lightweight policy which consists of a sequence of state-action pairs to influence conversational behaviours without training. Our framework regards prompts as actions executed by LLMs, and dynamically constructs prompts through five components based on the current state of the agent. To test the effectiveness of parameterized control, we evaluated the dialogue flow based on five indicators: responsiveness, rebuttal, evidence usage, non-repetition, and stance shift. We conduct experiments using different LLM-driven agents in two discussion scenarios related to the general public and show that prompt parameterization can influence the dialogue dynamics. This result shows that policy-parameterised prompts offer a simple and effective mechanism to influence the dialogue process, which will help the research of multi-agent systems in the direction of social simulation.

</details>


### [206] [MedMASLab: A Unified Orchestration Framework for Benchmarking Multimodal Medical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09909)
*Yunhang Qian,Xiaobin Hu,Jiaquan Yu,Siyang Xin,Xiaokun Chen,Jiangning Zhang,Peng-Tao Jiang,Jiawei Liu,Hongwei Bran Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedMASLab是一个统一的多模态医疗多智能体系统框架和基准测试平台，旨在解决当前医疗MAS研究中的架构碎片化、多模态集成标准化缺失以及跨专业基准测试不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗多智能体系统研究存在架构碎片化、缺乏标准化多模态集成、数据摄入管道不统一、视觉推理评估不一致以及跨专业基准测试缺失等问题，这些限制了MAS在复杂临床决策支持中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出MedMASLab框架，包含三个核心组件：1）标准化多模态智能体通信协议，支持11种异构MAS架构和24种医疗模态的无缝集成；2）自动化临床推理评估器，采用零样本语义评估范式，利用大型视觉语言模型验证诊断逻辑和视觉基础；3）迄今为止最广泛的基准测试，涵盖11个器官系统和473种疾病，标准化了11个临床基准数据。

Result: 系统评估揭示了关键的领域特定性能差距：虽然MAS提高了推理深度，但当前架构在跨专业医疗子领域转换时表现出显著脆弱性。研究提供了交互机制和成本-性能权衡的严格消融分析，为未来自主临床系统建立了新的技术基线。

Conclusion: MedMASLab通过提供统一的框架和基准测试平台，解决了医疗多智能体系统研究中的关键挑战，为未来自主临床系统的开发建立了标准化基础，并揭示了当前架构在跨专业医疗领域转换中的脆弱性，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: While Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) show potential for complex clinical decision support, the field remains hindered by architectural fragmentation and the lack of standardized multimodal integration. Current medical MAS research suffers from non-uniform data ingestion pipelines, inconsistent visual-reasoning evaluation, and a lack of cross-specialty benchmarking. To address these challenges, we present MedMASLab, a unified framework and benchmarking platform for multimodal medical multi-agent systems. MedMASLab introduces: (1) A standardized multimodal agent communication protocol that enables seamless integration of 11 heterogeneous MAS architectures across 24 medical modalities. (2) An automated clinical reasoning evaluator, a zero-shot semantic evaluation paradigm that overcomes the limitations of lexical string-matching by leveraging large vision-language models to verify diagnostic logic and visual grounding. (3) The most extensive benchmark to date, spanning 11 organ systems and 473 diseases, standardizing data from 11 clinical benchmarks. Our systematic evaluation reveals a critical domain-specific performance gap: while MAS improves reasoning depth, current architectures exhibit significant fragility when transitioning between specialized medical sub-domains. We provide a rigorous ablation of interaction mechanisms and cost-performance trade-offs, establishing a new technical baseline for future autonomous clinical systems. The source code and data is publicly available at: https://github.com/NUS-Project/MedMASLab/

</details>


### [207] [PathMem: Toward Cognition-Aligned Memory Transformation for Pathology MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09943)
*Jinyue Li,Yuci Liang,Qiankun Li,Xinheng Lyu,Jiayu Qian,Huabao Chen,Kun Wang,Zhigang Zeng,Anil Anthony Bharath,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathMem是一个用于病理学多模态大语言模型的记忆中心框架，通过组织结构化病理知识作为长期记忆，并引入记忆Transformer实现从长期记忆到工作记忆的动态转换，显著提升了病理诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算病理学需要视觉模式识别和结构化领域知识（如分类学、分级标准和临床证据）的动态整合。现有多模态大语言模型缺乏结构化知识集成和可解释记忆控制机制，难以在推理中一致地融入病理学特定的诊断标准。

Method: 受人类病理学家分层记忆过程启发，提出PathMem框架：1) 将结构化病理知识组织为长期记忆；2) 引入记忆Transformer，通过多模态记忆激活和上下文感知知识接地，建模从长期记忆到工作记忆的动态转换；3) 实现上下文感知记忆精炼以支持下游推理。

Result: 在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能：WSI-Bench报告生成任务中，WSI-Precision提升12.8%，WSI-Relevance提升10.1%；开放诊断任务中，相比先前基于WSI的模型分别提升9.7%和8.9%。

Conclusion: PathMem通过记忆中心框架有效整合了结构化病理知识，解决了现有模型在病理学推理中知识集成不足的问题，显著提升了诊断准确性和报告质量，为计算病理学提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Computational pathology demands both visual pattern recognition and dynamic integration of structured domain knowledge, including taxonomy, grading criteria, and clinical evidence. In practice, diagnostic reasoning requires linking morphological evidence with formal diagnostic and grading criteria. Although multimodal large language models (MLLMs) demonstrate strong vision language reasoning capabilities, they lack explicit mechanisms for structured knowledge integration and interpretable memory control. As a result, existing models struggle to consistently incorporate pathology-specific diagnostic standards during reasoning. Inspired by the hierarchical memory process of human pathologists, we propose PathMem, a memory-centric multimodal framework for pathology MLLMs. PathMem organizes structured pathology knowledge as a long-term memory (LTM) and introduces a Memory Transformer that models the dynamic transition from LTM to working memory (WM) through multimodal memory activation and context-aware knowledge grounding, enabling context-aware memory refinement for downstream reasoning. PathMem achieves SOTA performance across benchmarks, improving WSI-Bench report generation (12.8% WSI-Precision, 10.1% WSI-Relevance) and open-ended diagnosis by 9.7% and 8.9% over prior WSI-based models.

</details>


### [208] [The Confidence Gate Theorem: When Should Ranked Decision Systems Abstain?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09947)
*Ronald Doku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了基于置信度的弃权机制在排序决策系统中何时能单调提升决策质量，区分了结构性不确定性和上下文不确定性，并通过三个领域的实证验证了这一区分的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序决策系统（如推荐系统、广告拍卖、临床分诊队列）需要决定何时干预排序输出、何时弃权。当前缺乏对基于置信度的弃权机制何时能单调提升决策质量的理论理解，特别是不同不确定性类型（结构性vs上下文）对弃权效果的影响。

Method: 提出了形式化条件：排序对齐和无反转区域。通过区分结构性不确定性（缺失数据，如冷启动）和上下文不确定性（缺失上下文，如时间漂移）来解释这些条件何时成立或失败。在三个领域进行实证验证：协同过滤（MovieLens，3种分布偏移）、电商意图检测（RetailRocket, Criteo, Yoochoose）和临床路径分诊（MIMIC-IV）。比较了不同置信信号（观测计数、集成分歧、近期特征）的效果。

Result: 结构性不确定性在所有领域都产生近乎单调的弃权增益；基于结构的置信信号（观测计数）在上下文漂移下失效，在MovieLens时间分割上产生的单调性违规与随机弃权一样多。上下文感知替代方案（集成分歧和近期特征）显著缩小差距（违规从3减少到1-2），但未完全恢复单调性。基于残差的异常标签在分布偏移下性能大幅下降（AUC从0.71降至0.61-0.62）。

Conclusion: 基于置信度的弃权机制的效果取决于不确定性类型：结构性不确定性支持单调增益，而上下文不确定性带来本质不同的挑战。提供了实用的部署诊断：在部署置信门之前检查C1和C2条件，并将置信信号与主导不确定性类型匹配。异常标签方法在分布偏移下效果不佳。

Abstract: Ranked decision systems -- recommenders, ad auctions, clinical triage queues -- must decide when to intervene in ranked outputs and when to abstain. We study when confidence-based abstention monotonically improves decision quality, and when it fails. The formal conditions are simple: rank-alignment and no inversion zones. The substantive contribution is identifying why these conditions hold or fail: the distinction between structural uncertainty (missing data, e.g., cold-start) and contextual uncertainty (missing context, e.g., temporal drift). Empirically, we validate this distinction across three domains: collaborative filtering (MovieLens, 3 distribution shifts), e-commerce intent detection (RetailRocket, Criteo, Yoochoose), and clinical pathway triage (MIMIC-IV). Structural uncertainty produces near-monotonic abstention gains in all domains; structurally grounded confidence signals (observation counts) fail under contextual drift, producing as many monotonicity violations as random abstention on our MovieLens temporal split. Context-aware alternatives -- ensemble disagreement and recency features -- substantially narrow the gap (reducing violations from 3 to 1--2) but do not fully restore monotonicity, suggesting that contextual uncertainty poses qualitatively different challenges. Exception labels defined from residuals degrade substantially under distribution shift (AUC drops from 0.71 to 0.61--0.62 across three splits), providing a clean negative result against the common practice of exception-based intervention. The results provide a practical deployment diagnostic: check C1 and C2 on held-out data before deploying a confidence gate, and match the confidence signal to the dominant uncertainty type.

</details>


### [209] [Think Before You Lie: How Reasoning Improves Honesty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09957)
*Ann Yuan,Asma Ghandeharioun,Carter Blum,Alicia Machado,Jessica Hoffmann,Daphne Ippolito,Martin Wattenberg,Lucas Dixon,Katja Filippova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，与人类在深思熟虑后变得更不诚实不同，大型语言模型在推理过程中会变得更加诚实，这种效应与表征空间的几何结构有关，欺骗性区域具有亚稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注大型语言模型的欺骗率，但对导致欺骗行为的根本条件缺乏理解。本文旨在探究在现实道德权衡情境下，推理过程如何影响LLM的诚实行为。

Method: 使用包含诚实需要付出可变成本的现实道德权衡数据集进行研究。通过分析推理痕迹、输入改写、输出重采样和激活噪声等方法，探究表征空间的几何结构对诚实行为的影响。

Result: 研究发现推理过程会持续提高所有规模和多个LLM家族的诚实度。欺骗性答案在表征空间中处于亚稳定状态，更容易被输入改写、输出重采样和激活噪声破坏。推理通过在有偏的表征空间中生成审慎标记，最终将模型推向更稳定的诚实默认状态。

Conclusion: 与人类行为模式相反，推理过程使LLM变得更加诚实，这种效应源于表征空间的几何特性，其中欺骗性区域具有亚稳定性，而推理过程促使模型回归到更稳定的诚实默认状态。

Abstract: While existing evaluations of large language models (LLMs) measure deception rates, the underlying conditions that give rise to deceptive behavior are poorly understood. We investigate this question using a novel dataset of realistic moral trade-offs where honesty incurs variable costs. Contrary to humans, who tend to become less honest given time to deliberate (Capraro, 2017; Capraro et al., 2019), we find that reasoning consistently increases honesty across scales and for several LLM families. This effect is not only a function of the reasoning content, as reasoning traces are often poor predictors of final behaviors. Rather, we show that the underlying geometry of the representational space itself contributes to the effect. Namely, we observe that deceptive regions within this space are metastable: deceptive answers are more easily destabilized by input paraphrasing, output resampling, and activation noise than honest ones. We interpret the effect of reasoning in this vein: generating deliberative tokens as part of moral reasoning entails the traversal of a biased representational space, ultimately nudging the model toward its more stable, honest defaults.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Age-Related Differences in the Perception of Eye-Gaze from a Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08810)
*Lucas Morillo-Mendez,Martien G. S. Schrooten,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探索社交机器人的指示性凝视对协助老年人日常生活任务的好处，以及年龄相关差异如何影响老年人的社会感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社交机器人在辅助老年人日常生活任务中的应用增加，非语言线索如指示性凝视在自然的人机交互中变得重要。然而，老年人对指示性凝视的敏感性会自然下降，导致社会感知能力降低，因此需要研究年龄差异如何影响老年人对机器人指示性凝视的感知。

Method: 研究探索社交机器人的指示性凝视对协助老年人日常生活任务的好处，并比较老年人与年轻人群在社会感知方面的差异，以了解年龄相关因素如何影响人机交互中的非语言线索感知。

Result: 研究旨在揭示年龄差异如何影响老年人对社交机器人指示性凝视的社会感知，为设计适应年龄相关的非语言线索提供依据。

Conclusion: 理解年龄相关差异对老年人感知社交机器人指示性凝视的影响，有助于设计适应年龄特征的非语言线索，优化人机交互体验。

Abstract: There is an increasing interest in social robots assisting older adults during daily life tasks. In this context, non-verbal cues such as deictic gaze are important in natural communication in human-robot interaction. However, the sensibility to deictic-gaze declines naturally with age and results in a reduction in social perception. Therefore, this work explores the benefits of deictic gaze from social robots assisting older adults during daily life tasks, and how age-related differences may influence their social perception in contrast to younger populations. This may help on the design of adaptive age-related non-verbal cues in the Human-Robot Interaction context.

</details>


### [211] [Scale-Plan: Scalable Language-Enabled Task Planning for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08814)
*Piyush Gupta,Sangjae Bae,Jiachen Li,David Isele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Scale-Plan：一个可扩展的LLM辅助框架，通过构建动作图和使用浅层LLM推理来指导结构化图搜索，识别最小相关动作和对象子集，从而生成紧凑的任务相关问题表示，提升异构多机器人系统的长时程任务规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构多机器人系统的长时程任务规划面临挑战，因为大量感知信息中多数与任务目标无关，增加了规划负担。传统符号规划器依赖手动构建的问题规范，限制了可扩展性和适应性；而基于大语言模型的方法在对象丰富的环境中常出现幻觉和弱接地问题。

Method: 给定PDDL领域规范，Scale-Plan构建捕捉领域结构的动作图，使用浅层LLM推理指导结构化图搜索，识别最小相关动作和对象子集。通过在规划前过滤无关信息，实现高效分解、分配和长时程计划生成。

Result: Scale-Plan在所有指标上优于纯LLM和混合LLM-PDDL基线方法，提高了可扩展性和可靠性。研究还引入了MAT2-THOR基准，这是一个基于AI2-THOR构建的清洁基准，用于可靠评估多机器人规划系统。

Conclusion: Scale-Plan框架通过生成紧凑的任务相关问题表示，有效解决了异构多机器人系统长时程规划中的信息过载问题，结合了LLM的推理能力和符号规划的精确性，在复杂多智能体任务中表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon task planning for heterogeneous multi-robot systems is essential for deploying collaborative teams in real-world environments; yet, it remains challenging due to the large volume of perceptual information, much of which is irrelevant to task objectives and burdens planning. Traditional symbolic planners rely on manually constructed problem specifications, limiting scalability and adaptability, while recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches often suffer from hallucinations and weak grounding-i.e., poor alignment between generated plans and actual environmental objects and constraints-in object-rich settings. We present Scale-Plan, a scalable LLM-assisted framework that generates compact, task-relevant problem representations from natural language instructions. Given a PDDL domain specification, Scale-Plan constructs an action graph capturing domain structure and uses shallow LLM reasoning to guide a structured graph search that identifies a minimal subset of relevant actions and objects. By filtering irrelevant information prior to planning, Scale-Plan enables efficient decomposition, allocation, and long-horizon plan generation. We evaluate our approach on complex multi-agent tasks and introduce MAT2-THOR, a cleaned benchmark built on AI2-THOR for reliable evaluation of multi-robot planning systems. Scale-Plan outperforms pure LLM and hybrid LLM-PDDL baselines across all metrics, improving scalability and reliability.

</details>


### [212] [HMR-1: Hierarchical Massage Robot with Vision-Language-Model for Embodied Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08817)
*Rongtao Xu,Mingming Yu,Xiaofeng Han,Yu Zhang,Kaiyi Hu,Zhe Feng,Zenghuang Fu,Changwei Wang,Weiliang Meng,Xiaopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 构建了包含12,190张图像和174,177个问答对的MedMassage-12K多模态数据集，并提出包含高级穴位定位模块和低级控制模块的分层实体化按摩框架，建立了实体化按摩任务的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体智能在医疗保健领域快速发展，但在物理治疗和康复应用中面临关键挑战：缺乏标准化评估基准和开源多模态穴位按摩数据集。

Method: 1) 构建MedMassage-12K多模态数据集；2) 提出分层实体化按摩框架，包含高级穴位定位模块（使用多模态大语言模型理解人类语言并识别穴位位置）和低级控制模块（提供规划轨迹）；3) 基于此框架评估现有MLLMs并建立基准；4) 微调Qwen-VL模型验证框架有效性。

Result: 建立了包含12,190张图像和174,177个问答对的多模态数据集，提出了有效的分层实体化按摩框架，建立了实体化按摩任务的评估基准，并通过微调Qwen-VL模型和物理实验验证了框架的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建大规模多模态数据集和分层实体化按摩框架，解决了实体化医疗保健解决方案开发中的关键挑战，为实体化按摩任务提供了标准化评估基准，并通过实验验证了框架的实用价值。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Embodied Intelligence has opened transformative opportunities in healthcare, particularly in physical therapy and rehabilitation. However, critical challenges remain in developing robust embodied healthcare solutions, such as the lack of standardized evaluation benchmarks and the scarcity of open-source multimodal acupoint massage datasets. To address these gaps, we construct MedMassage-12K - a multimodal dataset containing 12,190 images with 174,177 QA pairs, covering diverse lighting conditions and backgrounds. Furthermore, we propose a hierarchical embodied massage framework, which includes a high-level acupoint grounding module and a low-level control module. The high-level acupoint grounding module uses multimodal large language models to understand human language and identify acupoint locations, while the low-level control module provides the planned trajectory. Based on this, we evaluate existing MLLMs and establish a benchmark for embodied massage tasks. Additionally, we fine-tune the Qwen-VL model, demonstrating the framework's effectiveness. Physical experiments further confirm the practical applicability of the framework.Our dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/Xiaofeng-Han-Res/HMR-1.

</details>


### [213] [Impact of Different Failures on a Robot's Perceived Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08821)
*Andrew Violette,Zhanxin Wu,Haruki Nishimura,Masha Itkina,Leticia Priebe Rocha,Mark Zolotas,Guy Hoffman,Hadas Kress-Gazit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究机器人不同类型故障（失误、冻结、错误）对人类感知可靠性(PR)的影响差异，以及成功执行如何恢复PR，无需机器人明确的社会修复行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人故障会降低其感知可靠性(PR)，而PR与可信度相关。需要了解不同类型故障对PR的影响差异，以及PR如何通过成功执行自然恢复，无需机器人主动修复。

Method: 采用预注册的在线视频研究，参与者观看机器人执行拾取放置任务的视频。实验包含11种视频条件：5种故障类型（失误、冻结、3种错误）、5种故障后成功执行、仅成功执行。随后展示两个额外成功视频。通过参与者的投注模式（机器人vs抛硬币）和问卷调查进行定性分析。

Result: 错误类故障对PR的损害小于失误或冻结；某些错误甚至被感知为成功。故障后立即的成功执行与无故障的成功执行对PR有相同效果。成功执行能够恢复故障后的PR。

Conclusion: 不同类型机器人故障对感知可靠性的影响不同，失误和冻结比错误更需要修复。机器人成功执行能够有效恢复PR，为HRI中信任修复策略提供指导。

Abstract: Robots fail, potentially leading to a loss in the robot's perceived reliability (PR), a measure correlated with trustworthiness. In this study we examine how various kinds of failures affect the PR of the robot differently, and how this measure recovers without explicit social repair actions by the robot. In a preregistered and controlled online video study, participants were asked to predict a robot's success in a pick-and-place task. We examined manipulation failures (slips), freezing (lapses), and three types of incorrect picked objects or place goals (mistakes). Participants were shown one of 11 videos -- one of five types of failure, one of five types of failure followed by a successful execution in the same video, or a successful execution video. This was followed by two additional successful execution videos. Participants bet money either on the robot or on a coin toss after each video. People's betting patterns along with a qualitative analysis of their survey responses highlight that mistakes are less damaging to PR than slips or lapses, and some mistakes are even perceived as successes. We also see that successes immediately following a failure have the same effect on PR as successes without a preceding failure. Finally, we show that successful executions recover PR after a failure. Our findings highlight which robot failures are in higher need of repair in a human-robot interaction, and how trust could be recovered by robot successes.

</details>


### [214] [Predictive Control with Indirect Adaptive Laws for Payload Transportation by Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08831)
*Leila Amanzadeh,Taizoon Chunawala,Randall T. Fawcett,Alexander Leonessa,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于四足机器人鲁棒负载运输的分层规划与控制框架，结合模型预测控制与梯度下降自适应更新律，能够处理未知静态和动态负载


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在运输未知、变化的负载时面临建模不确定性挑战，需要开发能够适应负载变化的鲁棒控制框架

Method: 分层框架：高层采用间接自适应律估计降阶模型未知参数，结合MPC进行实时轨迹规划，包含凸稳定性约束；低层采用非线性全身控制器跟踪轨迹

Result: 数值验证显示机器人能运输高达109%（平坦地形）和91%（粗糙地形）体重的静态负载，以及73%体重的动态负载；性能优于普通MPC和L1 MPC；硬件实验证实了在室内外粗糙地形上的有效性

Conclusion: 该分层自适应MPC框架为四足机器人鲁棒负载运输提供了有效解决方案，能够处理负载变化、扰动和地形不确定性

Abstract: This paper formally develops a novel hierarchical planning and control framework for robust payload transportation by quadrupedal robots, integrating a model predictive control (MPC) algorithm with a gradient-descent-based adaptive updating law. At the framework's high level, an indirect adaptive law estimates the unknown parameters of the reduced-order (template) locomotion model under varying payloads. These estimated parameters feed into an MPC algorithm for real-time trajectory planning, incorporating a convex stability criterion within the MPC constraints to ensure the stability of the template model's estimation error. The optimal reduced-order trajectories generated by the high-level adaptive MPC (AMPC) are then passed to a low-level nonlinear whole-body controller (WBC) for tracking. Extensive numerical investigations validate the framework's capabilities, showcasing the robot's proficiency in transporting unmodeled, unknown static payloads up to 109% in experiments on flat terrains and 91% on rough experimental terrains. The robot also successfully manages dynamic payloads with 73% of its mass on rough terrains. Performance comparisons with a normal MPC and an L1 MPC indicate a significant improvement. Furthermore, comprehensive hardware experiments conducted in indoor and outdoor environments confirm the method's efficacy on rough terrains despite uncertainties such as payload variations, push disturbances, and obstacles.

</details>


### [215] [SEP-NMPC: Safety Enhanced Passivity-Based Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for a UAV Slung Payload System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08860)
*Seyedreza Rezaei,Junjie Kang,Amaldev Haridevan,Jinjun Shan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SEP-NMPC框架，结合严格无源性不等式和高阶控制屏障函数，为带吊挂载荷的四旋翼提供稳定性和安全性保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机在吊挂载荷穿越复杂环境时，同时保证稳定性和无碰撞飞行具有挑战性，现有方法缺乏形式化保证

Method: 安全增强型无源性非线性模型预测控制（SEP-NMPC）：1）嵌入严格无源性不等式确保稳定性；2）使用高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBFs）保证安全性；3）保持二次规划兼容性，在线实时求解

Result: 仿真和实物实验验证了稳定载荷运输、无碰撞轨迹生成和实时可行性，在所有测试场景中均表现良好

Conclusion: SEP-NMPC框架统一了基于无源性的闭环稳定性和基于HOCBF的安全性保证，为无人机吊挂载荷运输提供了形式化保障

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) is widely adopted for agile multirotor vehicles, yet achieving both stability and obstacle-free flight is particularly challenging when a payload is suspended beneath the airframe. This paper introduces a Safety Enhanced Passivity-Based Nonlinear MPC (SEP-NMPC) that provides formal guarantees of stability and safety for a quadrotor transporting a slung payload through cluttered environments. Stability is enforced by embedding a strict passivity inequality, which is derived from a shaped energy storage function with adaptive damping, directly into the NMPC. This formulation dissipates excess energy and ensures asymptotic convergence despite payload swings. Safety is guaranteed through high-order control barrier functions (HOCBFs) that render user-defined clearance sets forward-invariant, obliging both the quadrotor and the swinging payload to maintain separation while interacting with static and dynamic obstacles. The optimization remains quadratic-program compatible and is solved online at each sampling time without gain scheduling or heuristic switching. Extensive simulations and real-world experiments confirm stable payload transport, collision-free trajectories, and real-time feasibility across all tested scenarios. The SEP-NMPC framework therefore unifies passivity-based closed-loop stability with HOCBF-based safety guarantees for UAV slung-payload transportation.

</details>


### [216] [APPLV: Adaptive Planner Parameter Learning from Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08862)
*Yuanjie Lu,Beichen Wang,Zhengqi Wu,Yang Li,Xiaomin Lin,Chengzhi Mao,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: APLLV：一种利用视觉-语言-动作模型预测规划器参数的自适应规划器参数学习方法，在约束环境中实现更好的导航性能和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法需要环境特定的参数调优，端到端学习方法难以在受限空间实现精确控制，现有VLA模型在导航任务中存在精确控制和推理延迟问题，需要一种能结合经典规划器安全保证和泛化能力的方法

Method: APLLV利用预训练的视觉-语言模型配合回归头来预测配置经典规划器的参数，而非直接输出动作。开发了两种训练策略：从收集的导航轨迹进行监督学习微调，以及进一步优化导航性能的强化学习微调

Result: 在模拟BARN数据集和物理机器人实验中，APLLV在多个运动规划器上均优于现有方法，在导航性能和未见环境泛化方面表现更佳

Conclusion: APLLV通过将视觉-语言模型的场景理解能力与经典规划器的安全保证相结合，实现了在高度约束环境中更好的自主导航性能和泛化能力

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in highly constrained environments remains challenging for mobile robots. Classical navigation approaches offer safety assurances but require environment-specific parameter tuning; end-to-end learning bypasses parameter tuning but struggles with precise control in constrained spaces. To this end, recent robot learning approaches automate parameter tuning while retaining classical systems' safety, yet still face challenges in generalizing to unseen environments. Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promise by leveraging foundation models' scene understanding capabilities, but still struggle with precise control and inference latency in navigation tasks. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Planner Parameter Learning from Vision-Language-Action Model (\textsc{applv}). Unlike traditional VLA models that directly output actions, \textsc{applv} leverages pre-trained vision-language models with a regression head to predict planner parameters that configure classical planners. We develop two training strategies: supervised learning fine-tuning from collected navigation trajectories and reinforcement learning fine-tuning to further optimize navigation performance. We evaluate \textsc{applv} across multiple motion planners on the simulated Benchmark Autonomous Robot Navigation (BARN) dataset and in physical robot experiments. Results demonstrate that \textsc{applv} outperforms existing methods in both navigation performance and generalization to unseen environments.

</details>


### [217] [Adaptive SINDy: Residual Force System Identification Based UAV Disturbance Rejection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08863)
*Fawad Mehboob,Amir Atef Habel,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Clement Fortin,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将数据驱动的SINDy系统辨识与RLS自适应控制相结合的方法，用于无人机在湍流环境中的风扰动抑制和轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在湍流环境中的稳定与控制是一个重要问题。传统方法（如扰动观测器和经典自适应控制）在复杂环境中表现有限，而基于学习的方法虽然应用增多，但其泛化性和可解释性存在担忧。需要一种既能有效抑制风扰动又具有良好泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的集成方法：使用稀疏非线性动力学辨识（SINDy）进行数据驱动的系统辨识，结合递归最小二乘（RLS）自适应控制来适应和抑制湍流环境中的风扰动。该方法能够在线适应动态变化的风场。

Result: 在Gazebo谐波环境和真实飞行中进行了测试验证，风速高达2 m/s，来自四个方向，创造了高度动态的湍流环境。自适应SINDy在多个轨迹跟踪误差指标上优于基线PID和INDI控制器，且未发生坠毁。在圆形和双纽线轨迹上分别实现了高达12.2 cm和17.6 cm的RMSE，以及13.7 cm和10.5 cm的MAE。验证在非常轻量的Crazyflie无人机上进行，用于复杂轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应SINDy方法在高度动态的湍流环境中表现出优越的轨迹跟踪性能，相比传统控制器具有更好的扰动抑制能力，为无人机在复杂风环境中的控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The stability and control of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) in a turbulent environment is a matter of great concern. Devising a robust control algorithm to reject disturbances is challenging due to the highly nonlinear nature of wind dynamics, and modeling the dynamics using analytical techniques is not straightforward. While traditional techniques using disturbance observers and classical adaptive control have shown some progress, they are mostly limited to relatively non-complex environments. On the other hand, learning based approaches are increasingly being used for modeling of residual forces and disturbance rejection; however, their generalization and interpretability is a factor of concern. To this end, we propose a novel integration of data-driven system identification using Sparse Identification of Non-Linear Dynamics (SINDy) with a Recursive Least Square (RLS) adaptive control to adapt and reject wind disturbances in a turbulent environment. We tested and validated our approach on Gazebo harmonic environment and on real flights with wind speeds of up to 2 m/s from four directions, creating a highly dynamic and turbulent environment. Adaptive SINDy outperformed the baseline PID and INDI controllers on several trajectory tracking error metrics without crashing. A root mean square error (RMSE) of up to 12.2 cm and 17.6 cm, and a mean absolute error (MAE) of 13.7 cm and 10.5 cm were achieved on circular and lemniscate trajectories, respectively. The validation was performed on a very lightweight Crazyflie drone under a highly dynamic environment for complex trajectory tracking.

</details>


### [218] [Proprioceptive Safe Active Navigation and Exploration for Planetary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08905)
*Matthew Y. Jiang,Feifei Qian,Shipeng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PSANE框架利用腿式机器人腿-地形交互的感知信息，通过高斯过程回归学习可通行性模型，实现未知可变形环境中的安全导航与探索


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形颗粒地形在行星探测中带来显著的运动和固定风险，且难以通过遥感（如视觉）检测。腿式机器人可以通过腿-地形交互感知地形特性，但如何系统利用这种交互信息进行导航规划仍待探索

Method: 提出PSANE框架：1）通过高斯过程回归学习可通行性模型，在线估计和认证安全区域并识别探索前沿；2）将估计与反应式控制器集成实现实时导航；3）前沿选择建模为多目标优化，平衡安全集扩展概率和目标导向成本；4）通过标量化在帕累托最优前沿集中选择子目标

Result: PSANE能够仅使用本体感知估计的可通行性安全探索未知颗粒地形并到达指定目标，相比基线方法实现了性能提升

Conclusion: PSANE框架成功展示了如何系统利用腿-地形交互信息进行导航规划，为可变形环境中的机器人安全探索提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Deformable granular terrains introduce significant locomotion and immobilization risks in planetary exploration and are difficult to detect via remote sensing (e.g., vision). Legged robots can sense terrain properties through leg-terrain interactions during locomotion, offering a direct means to assess traversability in deformable environments. How to systematically exploit this interaction-derived information for navigation planning, however, remains underexplored. We address this gap by presenting PSANE, a Proprioceptive Safe Active Navigation and Exploration framework that leverages leg-terrain interaction measurements for safe navigation and exploration in unknown deformable environments. PSANE learns a traversability model via Gaussian Process regression to estimate and certify safe regions and identify exploration frontiers online, and integrates these estimates with a reactive controller for real-time navigation. Frontier selection is formulated as a multi-objective optimization that balances safe-set expansion probability and goal-directed cost, with subgoals selected via scalarization over the Pareto-optimal frontier set. PSANE safely explores unknown granular terrain and reaches specified goals using only proprioceptively estimated traversability, while achieving performance improvements over baseline methods.

</details>


### [219] [Formation-Aware Adaptive Conformalized Perception for Safe Leader-Follower Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08958)
*Richie R. Suganda,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险感知Mondrian CP的分布式、编队感知自适应共形预测方法，用于解决分布式视觉编队中的感知安全问题，通过编队感知的CBF-QP保证可见性约束，提高编队成功率和跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式视觉编队中，机器人使用机载感知估计相对状态、跟踪期望设定点并保持领导者在相机视野内。由于异方差感知误差以及编队机动与可见性约束之间的耦合，安全问题具有挑战性。

Method: 提出基于风险感知Mondrian CP的分布式编队感知自适应共形预测方法，生成编队条件不确定性分位数。将这些边界集成到具有平滑裕度的编队感知共形CBF-QP中，以强制执行可见性约束，同时保持可行性和跟踪性能。

Result: Gazebo仿真显示，相比于忽略编队相关可见性风险的非自适应（全局）CP基线方法，该方法提高了编队成功率和跟踪精度，同时保持了有限样本概率安全保证。

Conclusion: 所提出的编队感知自适应共形预测方法能有效处理分布式视觉编队中的感知安全问题，通过动态调整不确定性边界来适应不同风险配置，在保证安全性的同时提升系统性能。

Abstract: This paper considers the perception safety problem in distributed vision-based leader-follower formations, where each robot uses onboard perception to estimate relative states, track desired setpoints, and keep the leader within its camera field of view (FOV). Safety is challenging due to heteroscedastic perception errors and the coupling between formation maneuvers and visibility constraints. We propose a distributed, formation-aware adaptive conformal prediction method based on Risk-Aware Mondrian CP to produce formation-conditioned uncertainty quantiles. The resulting bounds tighten in high-risk configurations (near FOV limits) and relax in safer regions. We integrate these bounds into a Formation-Aware Conformal CBF-QP with a smooth margin to enforce visibility while maintaining feasibility and tracking performance. Gazebo simulations show improved formation success rates and tracking accuracy over non-adaptive (global) CP baselines that ignore formation-dependent visibility risk, while preserving finite-sample probabilistic safety guarantees. The experimental videos are available on the \href{https://nail-uh.github.io/iros2026.github.io/}{project website}\footnote{Project Website: https://nail-uh.github.io/iros2026.github.io/}.

</details>


### [220] [FAME: Force-Adaptive RL for Expanding the Manipulation Envelope of a Full-Scale Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08961)
*Niraj Pudasaini,Yutong Zhang,Jensen Lavering,Alessandro Roncone,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME是一个力自适应强化学习框架，通过编码上半身关节配置和双手交互力来增强人形机器人在双手机器人操作中的平衡能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人双手机器人操作中，外部手部力会通过运动链传播并约束可行的操作范围，需要一种能够适应交互力的平衡控制方法

Method: 提出FAME框架，将站立策略条件化于学习的潜在上下文编码，该编码包含上半身关节配置和双手交互力；训练时在仿真中施加多样化的3D力扰动和上半身姿态课程；部署时从机器人动力学估计交互力

Result: 在仿真中，FAME将平均站立成功率提升至73.84%，显著优于仅使用课程的基线（51.40%）和基础策略（29.44%）；在Unitree H12人形机器人上成功部署，在非对称单臂负载和对称双手负载场景中表现出鲁棒性

Conclusion: FAME通过力自适应强化学习有效增强了人形机器人在双手机器人操作中的平衡鲁棒性，无需腕部力/力矩传感器即可在线适应交互力

Abstract: Maintaining balance under external hand forces is critical for humanoid bimanual manipulation, where interaction forces propagate through the kinematic chain and constrain the feasible manipulation envelope. We propose \textbf{FAME}, a force-adaptive reinforcement learning framework that conditions a standing policy on a learned latent context encoding upper-body joint configuration and bimanual interaction forces. During training, we apply diverse, spherically sampled 3D forces on each hand to inject disturbances in simulation together with an upper-body pose curriculum, exposing the policy to manipulation-induced perturbations across continuously varying arm configurations. At deployment, interaction forces are estimated from the robot dynamics and fed to the same encoder, enabling online adaptation without wrist force/torque sensors. In simulation across five fixed arm configurations with randomized hand forces and commanded base heights, FAME improves mean standing success to 73.84%, compared to 51.40% for the curriculum-only baseline and 29.44% for the base policy. We further deploy the learned policy on a full-scale Unitree H12 humanoid and evaluate robustness in representative load-interaction scenarios, including asymmetric single-arm load and symmetric bimanual load. Code and videos are available on https://fame10.github.io/Fame/

</details>


### [221] [SurgCalib: Gaussian Splatting-Based Hand-Eye Calibration for Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08983)
*Zijian Wu,Shuojue Yang,Yu Chung Lee,Eitan Prisman,Yueming Jin,Septimiu E. Salcudean*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯溅射的达芬奇手术机器人手眼标定框架SurgCalib，实现无标记自动标定，解决手术室中传统标定方法需要标记物的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉引导的机器人系统中，准确估计机器人基座与相机坐标系之间的刚性变换对于可靠的闭环控制至关重要。对于线驱动手术机器人，由于电缆拉伸和回差导致编码器测量不准确，且传统手眼标定方法需要已知标记物，这在手术室环境中可能违反无菌协议并干扰手术流程。

Method: 提出SurgCalib框架：首先使用原始运动学测量初始化手术器械姿态，然后在基于高斯溅射的可微分渲染管道中，通过两阶段优化程序在RCM约束下细化姿态。

Result: 在公开的dVRK基准SurgPose上评估：左器械2D工具尖端重投影误差12.24像素（2.06毫米），右器械11.33像素（1.9毫米）；左器械3D工具尖端欧氏距离误差5.98毫米，右器械4.75毫米。

Conclusion: SurgCalib是一种自动、无标记的手眼标定框架，具有在手术室中应用的潜力，能够在不引入额外标记物的情况下实现准确标定。

Abstract: We present a Gaussian Splatting-based framework for hand-eye calibration of the da Vinci surgical robot. In a vision-guided robotic system, accurate estimation of the rigid transformation between the robot base and the camera frame is essential for reliable closed-loop control. For cable-driven surgical robots, this task faces unique challenges. The encoders of surgical instruments often produce inaccurate proprioceptive measurements due to cable stretch and backlash. Conventional hand-eye calibration approaches typically rely on known fiducial patterns and solve the AX = XB formulation. While effective, introducing additional markers into the operating room (OR) environment can violate sterility protocols and disrupt surgical workflows. In this study, we propose SurgCalib, an automatic, markerless framework that has the potential to be used in the OR. SurgCalib first initializes the pose of the surgical instrument using raw kinematic measurements and subsequently refines this pose through a two-phase optimization procedure under the RCM constraint within a Gaussian Splatting-based differentiable rendering pipeline. We evaluate the proposed method on the public dVRK benchmark, SurgPose. The results demonstrate average 2D tool-tip reprojection errors of 12.24 px (2.06 mm) and 11.33 px (1.9 mm), and 3D tool-tip Euclidean distance errors of 5.98 mm and 4.75 mm, for the left and right instruments, respectively.

</details>


### [222] [Characterization, Analytical Planning, and Hybrid Force Control for the Inspire RH56DFX Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08988)
*Xuan Tan,William Xie,Nikolaus Correll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对Inspire RH56DFX灵巧手进行三项改进：硬件表征、仿真模型验证和混合控制算法，将其从黑盒设备转变为研究工具，在插孔任务中达到65%成功率，在300次抓取中达到87%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业灵巧手如Inspire RH56DFX存在多个问题：仅提供未校准的本体感知信息、高速接触行为不可靠（力限制超调达1618%）、欠驱动耦合连杆使对握抓取困难，难以作为科学研究工具使用。

Method: 提出三项改进：1) 硬件表征（力校准、延迟和超调测量）；2) 基于MuJoCo的sim2real验证模型，用于宽度到抓取的解析规划；3) 混合闭环速度-力抓取控制器。方法模块化设计，兼容外部物体检测器和视觉语言模型。

Result: 在插孔任务中达到65%成功率，显著优于仅使用腕力的基线（10%）；在15种物理多样性物体的300次抓取中达到87%成功率，优于无规划抓取和学习抓取方法。

Conclusion: 成功将Inspire RH56DFX从黑盒设备转变为研究工具，提供可解释且可立即部署的灵巧操作接口，开源实现为相关研究提供实用平台。

Abstract: Commercially accessible dexterous robot hands are increasingly prevalent, but many remain difficult to use as scientific instruments. For example, the Inspire RH56DFX hand exposes only uncalibrated proprioceptive information and shows unreliable contact behavior at high speed (up to 1618% force limit overshoot). Furthermore, its underactuated, coupled finger linkages make antipodal grasps non-trivial. We contribute three improvements to the Inspire RH56DFX to transform it from a black-box device to a research tool: (1) hardware characterization (force calibration, latency, and overshoot), (2) a sim2real validated MuJoCo model for analytical width-to-grasp planning, and (3) a hybrid, closed-loop speed-force grasp controller. We validate these components on peg-in-hole insertion, achieving 65% success and outperforming a wrist-force-only baseline of 10% and on 300 grasps across 15 physically diverse objects, achieving 87% success and outperforming plan-free grasps and learned grasps. Our approach is modular, designed for compatibility with external object detectors and vision-language models for width & force estimation and high-level planning, and provides an interpretable and immediately deployable interface for dexterous manipulation with the Inspire RH56DFX hand, open-sourced at this website https://correlllab.github.io/rh56dfx.html.

</details>


### [223] [Improving through Interaction: Searching Behavioral Representation Spaces with CMA-ES-IG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09011)
*Nathaniel Dennler,Zhonghao Shi,Yiran Tao,Andreea Bobu,Stefanos Nikolaidis,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CMA-ES-IG算法，将用户体验考虑融入机器人偏好学习过程，通过推荐感知上不同且信息丰富的轨迹供用户排序，提高非专家用户的采用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人偏好学习技术主要关注优化学习结果（如样本效率或偏好估计精度），但忽视了用户在提供排序过程中的关键期望，这会影响用户对机器人系统的采用。需要将用户体验考虑纳入偏好学习过程。

Method: 提出协方差矩阵自适应进化策略与信息增益（CMA-ES-IG）算法，该算法通过建议感知上不同且信息丰富的轨迹供用户排序，在偏好学习过程中明确纳入用户体验考虑。

Result: 通过仿真研究和真实机器人实验验证，CMA-ES-IG相比最先进方法：（1）能更有效地扩展到高维偏好空间；（2）对高维问题保持计算可行性；（3）对噪声或不一致的用户反馈具有鲁棒性；（4）非专家用户更倾向于使用它来识别偏好的机器人行为。

Conclusion: CMA-ES-IG算法成功地将用户体验考虑融入机器人偏好学习过程，通过提供感知上不同且信息丰富的查询，改善了用户参与度和系统采用率，同时保持了技术优势。

Abstract: Robots that interact with humans must adapt to individual users' preferences to operate effectively in human-centered environments. An intuitive and effective technique to learn non-expert users' preferences is through rankings of robot behaviors, e.g., trajectories, gestures, or voices. Existing techniques primarily focus on generating queries that optimize preference learning outcomes, such as sample efficiency or final preference estimation accuracy. However, the focus on outcome overlooks key user expectations in the process of providing these rankings, which can negatively impact users' adoption of robotic systems. This work proposes the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategies with Information Gain (CMA-ES-IG) algorithm. CMA-ES-IG explicitly incorporates user experience considerations into the preference learning process by suggesting perceptually distinct and informative trajectories for users to rank. We demonstrate these benefits through both simulated studies and real-robot experiments. CMA-ES-IG, compared to state-of-the-art alternatives, (1) scales more effectively to higher-dimensional preference spaces, (2) maintains computational tractability for high-dimensional problems, (3) is robust to noisy or inconsistent user feedback, and (4) is preferred by non-expert users in identifying their preferred robot behaviors. This project's code is available at github.com/interaction-lab/CMA-ES-IG

</details>


### [224] [PlayWorld: Learning Robot World Models from Autonomous Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09030)
*Tenny Yin,Zhiting Mei,Zhonghe Zheng,Miyu Yamane,David Wang,Jade Sceats,Samuel M. Bateman,Lihan Zha,Apurva Badithela,Ola Shorinwa,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PlayWorld：一种从无监督机器人自玩中学习高质量视频世界模拟器的自主流水线，显著提升物理一致性和策略性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于动作的视频模型在预测物理一致的机器人-物体交互方面存在困难，而现有方法依赖于有偏的人类演示数据，无法捕捉复杂的长尾物理交互

Method: 提出PlayWorld流水线，通过完全无监督的机器人自玩进行训练，实现可扩展的数据收集，能够捕捉复杂的物理交互和物体动力学

Result: 在多样化操作任务中生成高质量、物理一致的预测；在细粒度失败预测和策略评估方面比人类收集数据提升40%；在真实世界中部署时策略成功率提升65%

Conclusion: PlayWorld通过无监督自玩学习能够训练出高质量的物理一致视频世界模拟器，显著优于基于人类演示的方法，为机器人模拟和策略学习提供了新途径

Abstract: Action-conditioned video models offer a promising path to building general-purpose robot simulators that can improve directly from data. Yet, despite training on large-scale robot datasets, current state-of-the-art video models still struggle to predict physically consistent robot-object interactions that are crucial in robotic manipulation. To close this gap, we present PlayWorld, a simple, scalable, and fully autonomous pipeline for training high-fidelity video world simulators from interaction experience. In contrast to prior approaches that rely on success-biased human demonstrations, PlayWorld is the first system capable of learning entirely from unsupervised robot self-play, enabling naturally scalable data collection while capturing complex, long-tailed physical interactions essential for modeling realistic object dynamics. Experiments across diverse manipulation tasks show that PlayWorld generates high-quality, physically consistent predictions for contact-rich interactions that are not captured by world models trained on human-collected data.We further demonstrate the versatility of PlayWorld in enabling fine-grained failure prediction and policy evaluation, with up to 40% improvements over human-collected data. Finally, we demonstrate how PlayWorld enables reinforcement learning in the world model, improving policy performance by 65% in success rates when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [225] [ImpedanceDiffusion: Diffusion-Based Global Path Planning for UAV Swarm Navigation with Generative Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09031)
*Faryal Batool,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ImpedanceDiffusion：一种用于无人机群在杂乱室内环境导航的分层框架，结合图像条件扩散全局路径规划、APF跟踪和语义感知可变阻抗控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机群在杂乱室内环境中的安全导航问题，需要长时域规划、反应式避障和自适应柔顺性。传统方法在复杂动态环境中存在局限性，需要结合全局规划和局部反应控制的综合解决方案。

Method: 提出分层框架：1) 图像条件扩散模型直接从RGB图像生成全局轨迹，无需显式建图；2) APF反应层跟踪轨迹；3) VLM-RAG模块进行语义障碍物分类（90%检索准确率），自适应调整阻抗参数。评估两种扩散规划器：俯视图长时域规划器和第一人称视角短时域规划器。

Result: 两种规划器在20种静态和动态实验配置中均实现100%轨迹生成率。俯视图规划器产生更平滑轨迹，硬障碍物附近保守速度1.0-1.2 m/s，软障碍物0.6-1.0 m/s；FPV规划器轨迹局部间隙更大，速度更高（硬障碍物1.4-2.0 m/s，软障碍物最高1.6 m/s）。框架在100次运行中达到92%成功率，保持稳定阻抗编队控制，无飞行碰撞。

Conclusion: ImpedanceDiffusion框架通过结合扩散全局规划、APF反应跟踪和语义感知阻抗控制，实现了无人机群在杂乱室内环境中的可靠自适应导航，支持零样本仿真到真实部署，展示了分层方法在复杂导航任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Safe swarm navigation in cluttered indoor environment requires long-horizon planning, reactive obstacle avoidance, and adaptive compliance. We propose ImpedanceDiffusion, a hierarchical framework that leverages image-conditioned diffusion-based global path planning with Artificial Potential Field (APF) tracking and semantic-aware variable impedance control for aerial drone swarms.
  The diffusion model generates geometric global trajectories directly from RGB images without explicit map construction. These trajectories are tracked by an APF-based reactive layer, while a VLM-RAG module performs semantic obstacle classification with 90% retrieval accuracy to adapt impedance parameters for mixed obstacle environments during execution.
